```
110	/** 原版加速趋近模式（Projectile.cs 各宠物分支通用写法）：
111	 *  cur < want → +=acc（反向时再 +=acc）；cur > want → -=acc（对称） */
112	function accelToward(cur: number, want: number, acc: number): number {
113	  if (cur < want) {
114	    cur += acc;
115	    if (acc > 0.05 && cur < 0) cur += acc;
116	  } else if (cur > want) {
117	    cur -= acc;
118	    if (acc > 0.05 && cur > 0) cur -= acc;
119	  }
120	  return cur;

123	/** 实体盒是否嵌入实心块（越界视作实心——追赶飞行落回门的嵌块判定） */
124	function solidAtBox(st: { inBounds(c: number, r: number): boolean; isSolid(c: number, r: number): boolean },
125	  x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): boolean {
126	  const x0 = Math.floor(x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((x + w - 1) / TILE);
127	  const y0 = Math.floor(y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((y + h - 1) / TILE);
128	  for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
129	    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
130	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty) || st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
131	    }
132	  }
133	  return false;
134	}
135	
136	/** 跳跃梯取档（AI_026 :59293-59317 / AI_067 :47754-47780 同构）：按挡墙格头顶净空 */
137	function jumpPick(st: { inBounds(c: number, r: number): boolean; isSolid(c: number, r: number): boolean },
138	  tx: number, ty: number, ladder: number[]): number {
139	  const solid = (x: number, y: number) => st.inBounds(x, y) && st.isSolid(x, y);
140	  if (!solid(tx, ty - 1) && !solid(tx, ty - 2)) return ladder[0];  // -5.1
141	  if (!solid(tx, ty - 2)) return ladder[1];                        // -7.1
142	  if (solid(tx, ty - 5)) return ladder[2];                         // -11.1
143	  if (solid(tx, ty - 4)) return ladder[3];                         // -10.1
144	  return ladder[4];                                                // -9.1
145	}
146	
147	const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);
148	
149	// ===================== 实体 =====================
150	export class PetFollower extends Entity {
151	  /** 原版宠物全体走 Collision.StepUp（AI_026 :59280 / AI_067 :47756） */
152	  stepUp = true;
153	  w = 16;
154	  h = 16;
155	  dead = false;
156	  /** AI 参数（表外 id = null → legacy 回退） */
157	  private readonly params: PetParams | null;
158	  /** 地面族追赶飞行状态（原版 ai[0] != 0 分支 :57447） */
159	  private flying = false;
160	  /** 朝向（原版 direction；翻转经 flipSign 映射 spriteDirection） */
161	  private dir = 1;
162	  /** 动画帧与帧内计时（原版 frame/frameCounter） */
163	  private frame = 0;
164	  private frameAcc = 0;
165	  /** 慢速计时（bob 相位 / legacy 正弦浮动共用） */
166	  private animTick = 0;
167	  /** aiStyle 67 滚动角（rotation += vx/roll） */
168	  private rollRot = 0;
169	
170	  constructor(x: number, y: number, public readonly projId: number,
171	    private ownerRef: () => OwnerRef | null) {
172	    super();
173	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
174	    const pd = projectileData(projId);
175	    this.w = pd?.width ?? 16;
176	    this.h = pd?.height ?? 16;
177	    this.params = resolveParams(projId);
178	  }
179	
180	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
181	    const p = this.ownerRef();
182	    if (!p || p.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
183	    this.animTick++;
184	    const R = this.params;
185	    if (!R) { this.legacyAI(p); return; }   // 表外 id：回退登记见文件尾
186	    switch (R.family) {
187	      case 'ground': this.groundAI(p, game, R); break;
188	      case 'fly': this.flyAI(p, R); break;
189	      case 'hover': this.hoverAI(p, R); break;
190	    }
191	    this.stepAnim(R);
192	  }
193	
194	  // =============== 地面跳跃族（aiStyle 26 地面子型 + aiStyle 67 滚地族） ===============
195	  // AI_026 尾段（:59098-59440）+ ai[0]!=0 追赶飞行（:57447-57610）；
196	  // AI_067（:46471）同构，差异 = followDx 目标偏移 + roll 滚动角。
197	  private groundAI(p: OwnerRef, game: GameHooks, R: PetParams): void {
198	    const e = R.e;
199	    const D = TABLE.defaults.ground;
200	    const st = game.world.store;
201	    const pcx = p.cx, pcy = p.cy;
202	    const dx0 = pcx - this.cx, dy0 = pcy - this.cy;
203	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx0, dy0);
204	    const teleport = e.teleport ?? D.teleport ?? 2000;
205	
206	    // 超距瞬移（AI_026 :57368-57373 / AI_067 :47386-47388）
207	    if (dist > teleport) {
208	      this.x = pcx - this.w / 2; this.y = pcy - this.h / 2;
209	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0; this.flying = false;
210	      return;
211	    }
212	
213	    // 追赶飞行态（无重力直取玩家；AI_026 :57447-57610）
214	    if (this.flying) {
215	      const want = dist || 1;
216	      const flySpeed = e.flySpeed ?? D.flySpeed ?? 10;
217	      const flyAccel = e.flyAccel ?? D.flyAccel ?? 0.2;
218	      const tx = (dx0 / want) * flySpeed, ty = (dy0 / want) * flySpeed;
219	      this.vx = accelToward(this.vx, tx, flyAccel);
220	      this.vy = accelToward(this.vy, ty, flyAccel);
221	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
222	      // 落回地面门（:57556-57562）：距玩家 < landRange、玩家落地、宠物不高于玩家脚、
223	      // 宠物未嵌块 → 退出飞行（vy 钳 ≥ -6）
224	      const pvy = (p as { vy?: number }).vy ?? 0;
225	      const landRange = e.landRange ?? D.landRange ?? 200;
226	      if (dist < landRange && pvy === 0 && this.y + this.h <= p.y + p.h
227	        && !solidAtBox(st, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h)) {
228	        this.flying = false;
229	        if (this.vy < -6) this.vy = -6;
230	      }
231	      return;
232	    }
233	
234	    // 落后/被拉开 → 切追赶飞行（:57375-57405：dist > num91 或 |dy| > 300）
235	    const catchUpRange = e.catchUpRange ?? D.catchUpRange ?? 500;
236	    const catchUpVert = e.catchUpVert ?? D.catchUpVert ?? 300;
237	    if (dist > catchUpRange || Math.abs(dy0) > catchUpVert) {
238	      this.flying = true;
239	      return;
240	    }
241	
242	    // ---- 地面行走（:59187-59221）----
243	    const targetX = pcx + (e.followDx ?? 0) * p.facing;
244	    const dxs = targetX - this.cx;
245	    const idleRange = e.idleRange ?? D.idleRange ?? 85;
246	    const accel = e.accel ?? D.accel ?? 0.08;
247	    const maxSpeed = e.maxSpeed ?? D.maxSpeed ?? 6.5;
248	    if (dxs < -idleRange) {
249	      this.vx = this.vx > -3.5 ? this.vx - accel : this.vx - accel * 0.25;
250	    } else if (dxs > idleRange) {
251	      this.vx = this.vx < 3.5 ? this.vx + accel : this.vx + accel * 0.25;
252	    } else {
253	      this.vx *= 0.9;
254	      if (Math.abs(this.vx) <= accel) this.vx = 0;
255	    }
256	    this.vx = clamp(this.vx, -maxSpeed, maxSpeed);
257	    // 朝向（:59424-59432：direction = sign(velocity.X)，spriteDirection 由 flipSign 映射）
258	    if (this.vx < 0) this.dir = -1;
259	    else if (this.vx > 0) this.dir = 1;
260	
261	    // ---- 前方挡墙跳跃（:59256-59291 探墙 + :59293-59317 跳跃梯）----
262	    const moving = Math.abs(dxs) > idleRange && this.vx !== 0;
263	    if (this.onGround && moving) {
264	      const d = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
265	      const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE) + d + Math.floor(this.vx);
266	      const ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
267	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
268	        this.vy = jumpPick(st, tx, ty, e.jumpLadder ?? D.jumpLadder ?? [-5.1, -7.1, -11.1, -10.1, -9.1]);
269	      }
270	    }
271	
272	    // 重力 + 碰撞位移（:59421 通用档 velocity.Y += 0.4f / cap 10）
273	    const grav = e.gravity ?? D.gravity ?? 0.4;
274	    const fallCap = e.fallCap ?? D.fallCap ?? 10;
275	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + grav, fallCap);
276	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
277	    // aiStyle 67 滚动角（AI_067 :46795-46800：rotation += vx/20；1056/1090 /16）
278	    if (e.roll) this.rollRot += this.vx / e.roll;
279	  }
280	
281	  // =============== 飞行环绕族（aiStyle 26 悬停子型） ===============
282	  // 198 组共享体（:56468-56700）+ 175/197/199/211 独立分支。无 tileCollide。
283	  private flyAI(p: OwnerRef, R: PetParams): void {
284	    const e = R.e;
285	    const F = TABLE.defaults.fly;
286	    const hoverX = e.hoverX ?? F.hoverX ?? -60;
287	    const hoverY = e.hoverY ?? F.hoverY ?? -60;
288	    let tx = p.cx + hoverX * p.facing;
289	    let ty = p.cy + hoverY;
290	    if (e.jitter) {  // 每帧重掷目标点（:56495-56497 rand(-10,21)）
291	      tx += Math.random() * (e.jitter * 2 + 1) - e.jitter;
292	      ty += Math.random() * (e.jitter * 2 + 1) - e.jitter;
293	    }
294	    if (e.bob) {     // 774/1046 正弦浮游（:56489-56493）；702 余弦 bob（AI_144）
295	      const period = e.bobPeriod || 120;
296	      ty += Math.cos((this.animTick % period) / period * Math.PI * 2) * e.bob;
297	    }
298	    let dx = tx - this.cx, dy = ty - this.cy;
299	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
300	    const teleport = e.teleport ?? F.teleport ?? 2000;
301	    if (d > teleport) {  // 超距瞬移（:56526-56537）
302	      this.x = tx - this.w / 2; this.y = ty - this.h / 2;
303	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
304	      return;
305	    }
306	    const brake = e.brake ?? F.brake ?? 50;
307	    const mode = e.brakeMode ?? 'damp';
308	    let acc: number;
309	    if (d < brake) {
310	      switch (mode) {
311	        case 'direct':   // 199(:57021)/211(:56892)：贴身直接取位移
312	          this.vx = dx; this.vy = dy; acc = 0;
313	          break;
314	        case 'clamp': {  // 175(:56217-56225)/197：阻尼 + 位移钳 ±2
315	          if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 2 || Math.abs(this.vy) > 2) { this.vx *= 0.99; this.vy *= 0.99; }
316	          dx = clamp(dx, -2, 2); dy = clamp(dy, -2, 2);
317	          acc = 0.01;
318	          break;
319	        }
320	        default: {       // 198 组(:56647-56650)：阻尼 + 微加速度爬行
321	          if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 2 || Math.abs(this.vy) > 2) { this.vx *= 0.99; this.vy *= 0.99; }
322	          acc = 0.01;
323	          break;
324	        }
325	      }
326	    } else {
327	      const spd = (e.farSpeed !== undefined && e.farDist !== undefined && d > e.farDist)
328	        ? e.farSpeed : (e.speed ?? F.speed ?? 14);
329	      acc = (e.farAccel !== undefined && d > 300) ? e.farAccel : (e.accel ?? F.accel ?? 0.4);
330	      const k = spd / (d || 1);
331	      dx *= k; dy *= k;
332	    }
333	    if (acc > 0) {
334	      this.vx = accelToward(this.vx, dx, acc);
335	      this.vy = accelToward(this.vy, dy, acc);
336	    }
337	    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
338	    // 贴身钳 vy（:56537-56542，配合"玩家落地且宠物未嵌块"条件近似）
339	    const near = e.near ?? F.near ?? 100;
340	    if (d < near && this.vy < -6) this.vy = -6;
341	    // 朝向（:56686-56692：|vx|>0.25 阈值；199 恒 spriteDirection=1 由 flipSign=0 表达）
342	    if (this.vx > 0.25) this.dir = -1;
343	    else if (this.vx < -0.25) this.dir = 1;
344	  }
345	
346	  // =============== 悬停锚点族（aiStyle 11 / 90 / 124 / 144） ===============
347	  private hoverAI(p: OwnerRef, R: PetParams): void {
348	    const e = R.e;
349	    const H = TABLE.defaults.hover;
350	    const teleport = e.teleport ?? H.teleport ?? 1000;
351	
352	    // aiStyle 11（:22752-22804）：绕玩家保持 holdDist，速度恒定直取
353	    if (e.holdDist !== undefined) {
354	      const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - this.cy;
355	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
356	      if (d > teleport) {
357	        this.x = p.cx - this.w / 2; this.y = p.cy - this.h / 2;
358	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
359	      } else if (d > e.holdDist) {
360	        const k = (e.speed ?? 3) / (d || 1);
361	        this.vx = dx * k; this.vy = dy * k;
362	        this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
363	      } else {
364	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
365	      }
366	      return;
367	    }
368	
369	    const tx0 = p.cx + (e.hoverX ?? 0) * p.facing;
370	    let ty0 = p.cy + (e.hoverY ?? 0);
371	    if (e.bob) {
372	      const period = e.bobPeriod || 120;
373	      ty0 += Math.cos((this.animTick % period) / period * Math.PI * 2) * e.bob;  // AI_144 702 余弦 bob
374	    }
375	    const dx = tx0 - this.cx, dy = ty0 - this.cy;
376	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
377	    if (d > teleport) {   // 超距瞬移（124 :34149 / 144 :66804-66806 / 90 :31373）
378	      this.x = tx0 - this.w / 2; this.y = ty0 - this.h / 2;
379	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
380	      return;
381	    }
382	
383	    // aiStyle 90（:31377-31400）：速度斜坡 + 权重插值 v=(v*(n-1)+want)/n
384	    if (e.speedLerp !== undefined) {
385	      const base = e.speedBase ?? 4;
386	      let spd = base;
387	      if (e.rampDist !== undefined && e.ramp !== undefined && d > e.rampDist) {
388	        spd += (d - e.rampDist) * e.ramp;
389	      }
390	      if (d < spd) { this.vx *= 0.25; this.vy *= 0.25; spd = d; }
391	      const k = spd / (d || 1);
392	      const wx = dx * k, wy = dy * k;
393	      const n = e.speedLerp;
394	      this.vx = (this.vx * (n - 1) + wx) / n;
395	      this.vy = (this.vy * (n - 1) + wy) / n;
396	      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
397	      return;
398	    }
399	
400	    // 124/144（:34152-34169 / :66808-66819）：Δ < snap·0.5 直取，否则 vel = Δ×系数
401	    const snap = e.snap ?? H.snap ?? 4;
402	    const velLerp = e.velLerp ?? H.velLerp ?? 0.1;
403	    if (d < snap) { this.vx *= 0.25; this.vy *= 0.25; }
404	    if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) {
405	      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < snap * 0.5) { this.vx = dx; this.vy = dy; }
406	      else { this.vx = dx * velLerp; this.vy = dy * velLerp; }
407	    }
408	    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
409	  }
410	
411	  // ===================== 动画帧推进 =====================
412	  // 帧表 = 竖排条（Main.projFrames；帧高 = 图高/帧数）。逐款帧窗口（如 958 行走
413	  // 5-12/待机 0/空中 4）未逐款移植，按家族通用规则近似：
414	  //   ground：待机 0／空中 1／行走 0..min(frames,3) 按 |vx| 累进（:61700 通用式
415	  //           frameCounter += |vx|，>6 进帧）；
416	  //   fly：匀速循环全表（移动 4t/帧、待机 8t/帧）；
417	  //   hover：AI_144/124 原版双速率（待机 idleRate/移动 moveRate，vel>6 判移动）。
418	  private stepAnim(R: PetParams): void {
419	    const frames = Math.max(1, R.e.frames ?? 1);
420	    if (frames <= 1) { this.frame = 0; return; }
421	    switch (R.family) {
422	      case 'ground': {
423	        if (!this.onGround) { this.frame = Math.min(1, frames - 1); this.frameAcc = 0; break; }
424	        if (Math.abs(this.vx) <= 0.1) { this.frame = 0; this.frameAcc = 0; break; }
425	        this.frameAcc += Math.abs(this.vx);
426	        if (this.frameAcc > 6) {
427	          this.frameAcc = 0;
428	          const walk = Math.max(1, Math.min(frames, 3));
429	          this.frame = (this.frame + 1) % walk;
430	        }
431	        break;
432	      }
433	      case 'fly': {
434	        const rate = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 1 ? 4 : 8;
435	        if (++this.frameAcc >= rate) { this.frameAcc = 0; this.frame = (this.frame + 1) % frames; }
436	        break;
437	      }
438	      case 'hover': {
439	        const e = R.e;
440	        const rate = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 6
441	          ? (e.moveRate ?? 4) : (e.idleRate ?? 6);
442	        if (++this.frameAcc >= rate) {
443	          this.frameAcc = 0;
444	          const to = e.frameTo !== undefined ? Math.min(e.frameTo, frames - 1) : frames - 1;
445	          this.frame = this.frame >= to ? 0 : this.frame + 1;
446	        }
447	        break;
448	      }
449	    }
450	  }
451	
452	  // ===================== 绘制 =====================
453	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
454	    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Projectile_${this.projId}.png`);
455	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
456	    if (!ctx || !img) return;
457	    if (!this.params) { this.legacyDraw(ctx, img); return; }
458	    const frames = Math.max(1, this.params.e.frames ?? 1);
459	    // 竖排帧条（原版投射物表布局）：帧高 = 图高/帧数；贴图异常（不足一帧）回退整图
460	    const fh = frames > 1 ? img.height / frames : img.height;
461	    const useStrip = Number.isFinite(fh) && fh >= 1 && (frames <= 1 || img.height >= frames);
462	    const sx = 0, sy = useStrip ? Math.round(this.frame * fh) : 0;
463	    const sw = img.width, sh = useStrip ? Math.round(fh) : img.height;
464	    // 翻转 = 原版 spriteDirection（flipSign 映射：+1 朝右翻、-1 朝左翻、0 不翻）
465	    const face = this.params.e.faceFrom === 'player'
466	      ? (this.ownerRef()?.facing ?? 1)
467	      : this.dir;
468	    const flip = (this.params.e.flipSign ?? 0) !== 0
469	      && this.params.e.flipSign === Math.sign(face || 1);
470	    ctx.save();
471	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
472	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
473	    if (this.params.e.roll) ctx.rotate(this.rollRot);
474	    else if (flip) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
475	    // 帧按原像素尺寸 1:1 绘制（原版投射物 scale=1，判定盒小于贴图帧）
476	    ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh, -sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);
477	    ctx.restore();
478	  }
479	
480	  // =============== 回退：旧统一肩后悬停近似（表外 proj id 用） ===============
481	  // 登记缺口（本次升级后残留的近似项）：
482	  //  ① Wisp(211)/ShadowOrb(18) 的控制键偏移（controlUp/Down ±40、controlLeft/Right
483	  //    ±120）——ownerRef 无输入态，未建模；
484	  //  ② 764 UpbeatStar 透明度脉冲、1050 AxeFairyPet 砍树行为、653 同伴方块岩浆
485	  //    尖叫/黑暗戳人、1090 彩虹巨石微光粒子——纯视觉/彩蛋，未移植；
486	  //  ③ 地面族逐款帧窗口（958 行走 5-12、825 待机表情轮盘等）与 208 鹦鹉 0.95
487	  //    水平阻尼、1027 波浪帧段——按家族通用规则近似；
488	  //  ④ AI_026 抚摸(petting)交互帧、尘/Gore 特效未移植。
489	  private legacyAI(p: OwnerRef): void {
490	    // 目标位：玩家肩后（朝向反向 ×22px，头侧 -26px）+ 正弦浮动（±4px，0.06/t）
491	    const bob = Math.sin(this.animTick * 0.06) * 4;
492	    const tx = p.cx - p.facing * 22 - this.w / 2;
493	    const ty = p.y - 26 + bob;
494	    const dx = tx - this.x, dy = ty - this.y;
495	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
496	    if (dist > 1200) { this.x = tx; this.y = ty; return; }  // 远距瞬移近似
497	    const step = Math.min(6, dist);
498	    if (dist > 0.01) {
499	      this.x += (dx / dist) * step;
500	      this.y += (dy / dist) * step;
501	    }
502	  }
503	
504	  private legacyDraw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, img: HTMLImageElement): void {
505	    // 横排帧假设（旧版）：帧宽 = 贴图高（方形帧）；不足一帧的整图直接画
506	    const fw = img.height;
507	    const frames = fw > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.width / fw)) : 1;
508	    const fi = frames > 1 ? Math.floor(this.animTick / 8) % frames : 0;
509	    const p = this.ownerRef();
510	    const flip = p ? p.facing < 0 : false;
511	    ctx.save();
512	    if (flip) {
513	      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w, this.y);
514	      ctx.scale(-1, 1);
515	      ctx.drawImage(img, fi * fw, 0, fw, fw, 0, 0, this.w, this.h);
516	    } else {
517	      ctx.drawImage(img, fi * fw, 0, fw, fw, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
518	    }
519	    ctx.restore();
520	  }
521	}
522	

```

```
5	//   ① vanilla-pets.json 的 proj id 全集（唯一化）＋ vanilla-projectiles.json 的 aiStyle
6	//   ② Projectile.cs 的宠物 AI 方法体（AI_026 / AI_067_FreakingPirates / AI_144_DD2Pet
7	//      + AI() 内 aiStyle 11/90/124 内联分支）——参数带行号落盘
8	//   ③ Main.cs projFrames 表 + ProjectileID.cs 常量名
9	//
10	// 提取策略（与 extract-projectiles.mjs 同款语义模型）：
11	//   - 家族分类的飞行子型用"方法体顶层 if 分支扫描"实提（AI_026 里逐款专属分支
12	//     凡 depth-1 出现 tileCollide = false 即无重力悬停族）；
13	//   - 逐款数值用锚点定位 + 正则解析实提（idleRange/accel/maxSpeed/catchUpRange 等
14	//     全部可机械解析的都解析）；
15	//   - 少数完全定制的分支体（Wisp 控制偏移、DD2 宠 bob、764 星透明度脉冲等）以
16	//     常量表给出，但每个值都用 anchorAssert 校验源码对应行仍含锚文本——源码漂移
17	//     即报错退出，防止"表与源码静默失配"。
18	// 输出：src/data/vanilla-petai.json
19	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
20	
21	const ROOT = '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld';
22	const projSrc = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Projectile.cs`, 'utf8');
23	const mainSrc = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Main.cs`, 'utf8');
24	const idSrc = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ProjectileID.cs`, 'utf8');
25	const petsTable = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-pets.json`, 'utf8'));
26	const projTable = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-projectiles.json`, 'utf8'));
27	
28	const projLines = projSrc.split('\n');
29	/** 行号(1基) → 源码行 */
30	const lineAt = (n) => projLines[n - 1] ?? '';
31	/** 文本内偏移 → 1基行号 */
32	const lineNoOf = (text, idx) => text.slice(0, idx).split('\n').length;
33	
34	/** 平衡花括号取方法/分支体。from = 签名或 if 条件所在起点 */
35	function braceBody(src, from) {
36	  const open = src.indexOf('{', from);
37	  if (open < 0) return null;
38	  let d = 0;
39	  for (let i = open; i < src.length; i++) {
40	    if (src[i] === '{') d++;
41	    else if (src[i] === '}') { d--; if (d === 0) return { text: src.slice(open + 1, i), open }; }
42	  }
43	  return null;
44	}
45	/** 块内偏移 → 绝对行号定位器：blockStartLine = 块首字符在源码中的行号(1基) */
46	const locator = (block, blockStartLine) => (idx) => blockStartLine - 1 + lineNoOf(block, idx);
47	/** 平衡括号取 if 条件串 */
48	function readCond(src, parenAt) {
49	  let d = 0;
50	  for (let i = parenAt; i < src.length; i++) {
51	    if (src[i] === '(') d++;
52	    else if (src[i] === ')') { d--; if (d === 0) return { cond: src.slice(parenAt, i + 1), end: i + 1 }; }
53	  }
54	  return null;
55	}
56	/** 条件纯度：仅 type/数字/比较/逻辑/括号（AI_026 内 flag 变量与 ai 槽条件不可静态求值） */
57	function tryCompileTypeCond(cond) {
58	  const stripped = cond.replace(/\btype\b/g, '');
59	  if (!/^[\s\d()&|=<>!]+$/.test(stripped)) return null;
60	  try { return new Function('type', `return (${cond});`); } catch { return null; }
61	}
62	/** 块内 depth-1 行（花括号深度 0 的语句层） */
63	function depth1Lines(block) {
64	  const out = [];
65	  let d = 0;
66	  for (const raw of block.split('\n')) {
67	    const opens = (raw.match(/\{/g) || []).length;
68	    const closes = (raw.match(/\}/g) || []).length;
69	    if (d === 0) out.push(raw.trim());
70	    d += opens - closes;
71	    if (d < 0) d = 0;
72	  }
73	  return out;
74	}
75	/** 锚点校验：源码 line 行（1基）必须含 text——参数表的防漂移闸门 */
76	function anchorAssert(line, text, what) {
77	  const actual = lineAt(line);
78	  if (!actual.includes(text)) {
79	    throw new Error(`锚点失配 [${what}] Projectile.cs:${line} 期望含 "${text}" 实为 "${actual.trim()}"`);
80	  }
81	}
82	/** 块内首个匹配（line = projSrc 绝对行号） */
83	function firstIn(block, loc, re, what) {
84	  const m = re.exec(block);
85	  if (!m) throw new Error(`未找到 [${what}]`);
86	  return { value: m, line: loc(m.index) };
87	}
88	/** 块内全部匹配（lines = 绝对行号数组） */
89	function allIn(block, loc, re) {
90	  const out = [];
91	  let m;
92	  while ((m = re.exec(block))) { out.push({ value: m, line: loc(m.index) }); re.lastIndex = m.index + 1; }
93	  return out;
94	}
95	
96	// ================= ① 宠物 proj id 全集 + aiStyle =================
97	const petProjIds = [...new Set(Object.values(petsTable).map((e) => e.proj))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
98	const aiStyleOf = (id) => projTable[String(id)]?.aiStyle;
99	
100	// ================= ③ projFrames + 常量名 =================
101	const framesOf = {};
102	for (const m of mainSrc.matchAll(/projFrames\[(\d+)\] = (\d+);/g)) framesOf[+m[1]] = +m[2];
103	const nameOf = {};
104	for (const m of idSrc.matchAll(/public const short (\w+) = (\d+);/g)) {
105	  if (!(+m[2] in nameOf)) nameOf[+m[2]] = m[1];
106	}
107	
108	// ================= ② 方法体定位 =================
109	const methodAt = (sig) => {
110	  const i = projSrc.indexOf(sig);
111	  if (i < 0) throw new Error(`方法未找到: ${sig}`);
112	  const b = braceBody(projSrc, i);
113	  return { text: b.text, sigLine: lineNoOf(projSrc, i), loc: locator(b.text, lineNoOf(projSrc, b.open + 1)) };
114	};
115	const AI026 = methodAt('private void AI_026()');
116	const AI067 = methodAt('private void AI_067_FreakingPirates()');
117	const AI144 = methodAt('private void AI_144_DD2Pet()');
118	/** AI() 内联 aiStyle 分支（`else if (aiStyle == N) { ... }`） */
119	const inlineBranch = (n) => {
120	  const re = new RegExp(`else if \\(aiStyle == ${n}\\)`);
121	  const m = re.exec(projSrc);
122	  if (!m) throw new Error(`aiStyle ${n} 分支未找到`);
123	  const b = braceBody(projSrc, m.index);
124	  return { text: b.text, sigLine: lineNoOf(projSrc, m.index), loc: locator(b.text, lineNoOf(projSrc, b.open + 1)) };
125	};
126	const ST11 = inlineBranch(11);
127	const ST90 = inlineBranch(90);
128	const ST124 = inlineBranch(124);
129	
130	// ================= AI_026 顶层分支扫描：飞行子型判定 =================
131	// 语义：AI_026 前段的逐款专属分支凡 depth-1 出现 `tileCollide = false;` 即无重力
132	// 悬停（飞行环绕族）；其余 aiStyle 26 成员落入尾段地面行走+跳跃分支。
133	const flying26 = new Set();
134	{
135	  const body = AI026.text;
136	  const re = /\bif\s*\(/g;
137	  let m;
138	  while ((m = re.exec(body))) {
139	    let d0 = 0;
140	    for (let i = 0; i < m.index; i++) {
141	      if (body[i] === '{') d0++;
142	      else if (body[i] === '}') d0--;
143	    }
144	    if (d0 !== 0) continue; // 仅方法体语句层的 if（else-if 链的嵌套 if 不算）
145	    const c = readCond(body, m.index + m[0].length - 1);
146	    if (!c) break;
147	    const b = braceBody(body, c.end);
148	    if (!b) { re.lastIndex = c.end; continue; }
149	    const fn = tryCompileTypeCond(c.cond);
150	    if (fn) {
151	      const hits = petProjIds.filter((id) => { try { return !!fn(id); } catch { return false; } });
152	      if (hits.length && depth1Lines(b.text).includes('tileCollide = false;')) {
153	        hits.forEach((id) => flying26.add(id));
154	      }
155	    }
156	    re.lastIndex = c.end;
157	  }
158	}
159

161	const L = {}; // 参数行号登记
162	const g = {};
163	{
164	  // idleRange 头 + 逐款覆盖
165	  const head = firstIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /int num = 85;/, 'idleRange 默认');
166	  g.idleRange = 85; L.idleRange = head.line;
167	  const idleOv = {};
168	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num = (\d+);/g)) {
169	    idleOv[+value[1]] = +value[2];
170	  }
171	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /((?:case \d+:\s*)+)num = (\d+);/g)) {
172	    for (const id of [...value[1].matchAll(/case (\d+):/g)].map((x) => +x[1])) idleOv[id] = +value[2];
173	  }
174	  g.idleOverrides = idleOv;
175	  // accel/maxSpeed 头（num181/num182 相邻两行）
176	  const a0 = firstIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /float num181 = ([\d.]+)f;\n\s*float num182 = ([\d.]+)f;/, 'accel/maxSpeed 默认');
177	  g.accel = parseFloat(a0.value[1]); g.maxSpeed = parseFloat(a0.value[2]);
178	  L.accel = a0.line; L.maxSpeed = a0.line + 1;
179	  const spdOv = {};
180	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num182 = ([\d.]+)f;\s*num181 = ([\d.]+)f;/g)) {
181	    spdOv[+value[1]] = { maxSpeed: parseFloat(value[2]), accel: parseFloat(value[3]) };
182	  }
183	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num181 = ([\d.]+)f;\s*num182 = ([\d.]+)f;/g)) {
184	    spdOv[+value[1]] = { accel: parseFloat(value[2]), maxSpeed: parseFloat(value[3]) };
185	  }
186	  // 步速大组（flag10 || type == 816 || ... || type == 1096）→ 6 / 0.2
187	  {
188	    const m = /if \(flag10 \|\| type == 266[\s\S]{0,600}?type == 1096\)\s*\{\s*num182 = 6f;\s*num181 = 0\.2f;/.exec(AI026.text);
189	    if (!m) throw new Error('AI_026 步速大组未找到');
190	    for (const id of [...m[0].matchAll(/type == (\d+)/g)].map((x) => +x[1])) spdOv[id] = { maxSpeed: 6, accel: 0.2 };
191	    L.speedGroup = AI026.loc(m.index);
192	  }

196	    if (!m) throw new Error('AI_026 875 步速档未找到');

198	  }
199	  g.speedOverrides = spdOv;
200	  // catchUpRange 头 + 逐款/switch 覆盖
201	  const c0 = firstIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /int num91 = (\d+);/, 'catchUpRange 默认');
202	  g.catchUpRange = +c0.value[1]; L.catchUpRange = c0.line;
203	  const cuOv = {};
204	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num91 = (\d+);/g)) cuOv[+value[1]] = +value[2];
205	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /((?:case \d+:\s*)+)num91 = (\d+);/g)) {
206	    for (const id of [...value[1].matchAll(/case (\d+):/g)].map((x) => +x[1])) cuOv[id] = +value[2];
207	  }

210	  const f0 = firstIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /float num100 = ([\d.]+)f;\n\s*int num101 = (\d+);/, 'flyAccel/landRange 默认');
211	  g.flyAccel = parseFloat(f0.value[1]); g.landRange = +f0.value[2];
212	  L.flyAccel = f0.line; L.landRange = f0.line + 1;
213	  const s0 = firstIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /float num111 = ([\d.]+)f;/, 'flySpeed 默认');
214	  g.flySpeed = parseFloat(s0.value[1]); L.flySpeed = s0.line;
215	  const flyOv = {};
216	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num111 = ([\d.]+)f;/g)) {
217	    flyOv[+value[1]] = { ...(flyOv[+value[1]] || {}), flySpeed: parseFloat(value[2]) };
218	  }
219	  for (const { value } of allIn(AI026.text, AI026.loc, /if \(type == (\d+)\)\s*\{\s*num100 = ([\d.]+)f;\s*num111 = ([\d.]+)f;/g)) {
220	    flyOv[+value[1]] = { ...(flyOv[+value[1]] || {}), flyAccel: parseFloat(value[2]), flySpeed: parseFloat(value[3]) };
221	  }
222	  {
223	    const m = /if \(flag10 \|\| type == 816 [\s\S]{0,700}?type == 1096\)\s*\{\s*num100 = 0\.4f;\s*num111 = 12f;/.exec(AI026.text);
224	    if (!m) throw new Error('AI_026 追赶飞行大组未找到');
225	    for (const id of [...m[0].matchAll(/type == (\d+)/g)].map((x) => +x[1])) {
226	      flyOv[id] = { ...(flyOv[id] || {}), flyAccel: 0.4, flySpeed: 12 };
227	    }
228	  }
229	  {

231	    if (!m) throw new Error('AI_026 追赶降落大组未找到');
232	    for (const id of [...m[0].matchAll(/type == (\d+)/g)].map((x) => +x[1])) {

238	  }

241	  g.jumpLadder = [-5.1, -7.1, -11.1, -10.1, -9.1];

284	  774: { speed: 5, accel: 0.3 },   // SharkPup
285	  1046: { speed: 8, accel: 0.3 },  // Pufferfish（≥400 取 8）

288	  198: /float num53 = 14f;/,
289	  380: /if \(type == 380\)\s*\{\s*num53 = 6f;/,
290	  815: /if \(type == 815 \|\| type == 817\)\s*\{\s*num53 = \(\(!\(num51 < num44\)\) \? 10f : 6f\);/,
291	  817: /if \(type == 815 \|\| type == 817\)\s*\{\s*num53 = \(\(!\(num51 < num44\)\) \? 10f : 6f\);/,
```

```
204	// ===================== 运行时烟测 =====================
205	/** 测试用世界：row ≥ FLOOR 行为地板，WALL 列加一堵墙（跳跃用） */
206	function stubWorld(FLOOR: number, WALL: number | null) {
207	  const W = 400, H = 200, n = W * H;
208	  const slope = new Uint8Array(n), half = new Uint8Array(n);
209	  return {
210	    w: W, h: H, slope, half,
211	    inBounds: (c: number, r: number) => c >= 0 && c < W && r >= 0 && r < H,
212	    idx: (c: number, r: number) => r * W + c,
213	    isSolid: (c: number, r: number) => r >= FLOOR || (WALL !== null && c === WALL && r >= FLOOR - 10 && r < FLOOR),
214	    isPlatform: () => false,
215	  };
216	}
217	function stubGame(FLOOR = 60, WALL: number | null = null): GameHooks {
218	  return { world: { store: stubWorld(FLOOR, WALL) } } as unknown as GameHooks;
219	}
220	function stubPlayer(cx: number, floorRow: number, facing = 1) {
221	  const w = 20, h = 42, x = cx - w / 2, y = floorRow * 16 - h;
222	  return { x, y, w, h, cx, cy: y + h / 2, facing, dead: false };
223	}
224	
225	describe('PetFollower 三族引擎运行时', () => {
226	  it('ground 族（Bunny 111）：向玩家行走 + 前方挡墙触发跳跃梯', () => {
227	    const FLOOR = 60;
228	    const player = stubPlayer(110 * 16, FLOOR, 1);
229	    const game = stubGame(FLOOR, 95);   // col 95 一堵 10 格高的墙
230	    const pet = new PetFollower(90 * 16, FLOOR * 16 - 18, 111, () => player);
231	    const startX = pet.x, startY = pet.y;
232	    let minY = startY;
233	    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
234	      pet.fixedUpdate(1, game);
235	      minY = Math.min(minY, pet.y);
236	      expect(Number.isFinite(pet.x)).toBe(true);
237	      expect(Number.isFinite(pet.y)).toBe(true);
238	    }
239	    // 行走：向右靠近玩家（dist≈311 < catchUpRange 500 → 地面态）
240	    expect(pet.cx).toBeGreaterThan(startX + pet.w / 2 + 20);
241	    // 跳跃梯：前方挡墙（col 95 探测命中）→ vy 取 -5.1/-7.1/-9.1/-10.1/-11.1 档
242	    expect(minY).toBeLessThan(startY - 4);
243	  });
244	
245	  it('ground 族超距瞬移：dist > 2000 直接落在玩家身边', () => {
246	    const FLOOR = 60;
247	    const player = stubPlayer(100 * 16, FLOOR, 1);
248	    const game = stubGame(FLOOR, null);
249	    const pet = new PetFollower(100 * 16 + 3000, FLOOR * 16 - 18, 111, () => player);
250	    pet.fixedUpdate(1, game);
251	    expect(Math.abs(pet.cx - player.cx)).toBeLessThan(1);
252	    expect(pet.vy).toBe(0);
253	  });
254	
255	  it('fly 族（BabyHornet 198）：悬停玩家后上方并收敛（无 tileCollide）', () => {
256	    const player = stubPlayer(200 * 16, 60, 1);
257	    const game = stubGame(60, null);
258	    const pet = new PetFollower(150 * 16, 30 * 16, 198, () => player);
259	    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) pet.fixedUpdate(1, game);
260	    const wantX = player.cx - 60, wantY = player.cy - 60;   // hoverX -60 × facing
261	    expect(Math.hypot(pet.cx - wantX, pet.cy - wantY)).toBeLessThan(140);
262	    expect(Number.isFinite(pet.x)).toBe(true);
263	  });
264	
265	  it('hover 族（SuspiciousTentacle 650）：锚定 (30·facing, -20) 指数收敛', () => {
266	    const player = stubPlayer(200 * 16, 60, 1);
267	    const game = stubGame(60, null);
268	    const pet = new PetFollower(150 * 16, 30 * 16, 650, () => player);

274	  it('hover 族（ShadowOrb 18）：holdDist=70 死区——区外以速度 3 直取玩家中心收敛', () => {
275	    const player = stubPlayer(200 * 16, 60, 1);
276	    const game = stubGame(60, null);
277	    // 远距（>70）：每 tick 以速度 3 直取玩家中心（:22796-22800）→ 停在中心 ≤1 步内
278	    const pet = new PetFollower(150 * 16, 30 * 16, 18, () => player);
279	    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) pet.fixedUpdate(1, game);
280	    expect(Math.hypot(pet.cx - player.cx, pet.cy - player.cy)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(4);
281	    expect(pet.vx).toBe(0);
282	    // 死区内（<70）：不动（:22802-22804 velocity = 0）。proj 18 判定盒 32×32，
283	    // 构造位 (cx-40, cy-40) 的中心距 = hypot(40-16, 40-16) ≈ 34 < 70
284	    const pet2 = new PetFollower(player.cx - 40, player.cy - 40, 18, () => player);
285	    const d0 = Math.hypot(pet2.cx - player.cx, pet2.cy - player.cy);
286	    expect(d0).toBeLessThan(70);
287	    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) pet2.fixedUpdate(1, game);
288	    expect(Math.hypot(pet2.cx - player.cx, pet2.cy - player.cy)).toBeCloseTo(d0, 5);
289	    expect(pet2.vx).toBe(0);
290	  });
291	
292	  it('表外 proj id：回退旧肩后悬停近似（不崩、收敛、无 NaN）', () => {
293	    const player = stubPlayer(200 * 16, 60, 1);
294	    const game = stubGame(60, null);

299	  });
```

```
14	//                                                "本次完成"（_newlyUnlocked 语义）
15	//       MarkContentsDirty                 :219  LastEditId++
16	//   - CreativeItemSacrificesCatalog.cs:14-69    need 表加载（内嵌 Sacrifices.tsv）
17	//   - ContentSamples.cs:884-892                  CreativeResearchItemPersistentIdOverride
18	//                                                （牺牲品 id → 解锁目标 id，单步映射——
19	//                                                本版 8 组覆盖无链式，与原版一致）
20	//   - AchievementsHelper.cs CheckResearchAchievement :387-398
21	//       版本门（_lastResearchVersion != LastEditId 或 forced）才重数；
22	//       fullyResearchedItems >= allItems/2（C# 整除）→ NotifyProgressionEvent(45)
23	//       = 成就 TRASH_COMPACTOR（AchievementInitializer.cs:436-438）。
24	//       原版调用点：Player.OnEnterWorld forced:true（Player.cs:318）、
25	//       Player.Update 逐帧版本门（Player.cs:25418）。
26	//   - 成就 46（INTERDIMENSIONAL_RECYCLING）与研究无关：Main.cs:39721
27	//     DryadText_Do_StardewValleyBit（给树妖 Joja Cola 5275 的星露谷彩蛋）
28	//     → NotifyProgressionEvent(46)——勿接进本内核。
29	//
30	// 持久化：localStorage 键 sbw.research.v1（原版走 .wld CreativeTracker 二进制；
31	// 计数按「解析后 id」存，语义同 _sacrificesCountByItemIdCache，只是键型数字而非
32	// persistentId 字符串——override 映射确定性，二者等价【偏差登记】）。
33	import raw from '../data/vanilla-research.json';
34	
35	export interface ResearchStore {
36	  load(): string | null;
37	  save(s: string): void;
38	}
39	
40	export interface ResearchData {
41	  count: number;
42	  need: Record<string, number>;
43	  override: Record<string, number>;
44	}
45	
46	type Json = typeof raw;
47	const DATA = raw as Json as unknown as ResearchData;
48	
49	export const RESEARCH_STORE_KEY = 'sbw.research.v1';
50	
51	function localStorageStore(): ResearchStore {
52	  return {
53	    load: () => {
54	      try { return localStorage.getItem(RESEARCH_STORE_KEY); } catch { return null; }
55	    },
56	    save: (s) => { try { localStorage.setItem(RESEARCH_STORE_KEY, s); } catch { /* 满/无 DOM 忽略 */ } },
57	  };
58	}
59	
60	/** 原版 CountFullyResearchedItems 的返回对（ItemsSacrificedUnlocksTracker.cs:59-74） */
61	export interface SacrificeTotals { fullyResearchedItems: number; allItems: number }
62	
63	export class Research {
64	  /** 解析后 id → 累计献祭数（= _sacrificesCountByItemIdCache） */
65	  private counts = new Map<number, number>();
66	  private _lastEditId = 0;
67	  /** AchievementsHelper._lastResearchVersion（cs:385） */
68	  private achVersion = -1;
69	  /** NotifyProgressionEvent(45) 出口——Game 侧注入 ach.notifyProgressionEvent */
70	  onProgressionEvent: ((eventId: number) => void) | null = null;
71	
72	  constructor(
73	    private store: ResearchStore = localStorageStore(),
74	    private data: ResearchData = DATA,
75	  ) {
76	    this.load();
77	  }
78	
79	  // ---- 解析（ContentSamples.CreativeResearchItemPersistentIdOverride 单步）----
80	  private resolveId(itemId: number): number {
81	    const o = this.data.override[String(itemId)];
82	    return o === undefined ? itemId : o;
83	  }
84	
85	  // ---- 查询 ----
86	  /** 研究所需数量；0 = 不可研究（need 表无条目） */
87	  getNeed(itemId: number): number {
88	    return this.data.need[String(this.resolveId(itemId))] ?? 0;
89	  }
90	
91	  /** TryGetSacrificeNumbers（cs:76-89）：不可研究返回 null */
92	  tryGetSacrificeNumbers(itemId: number): { have: number; need: number } | null {
93	    const id = this.resolveId(itemId);
94	    const need = this.data.need[String(id)];
95	    if (need === undefined) return null;
96	    return { have: this.counts.get(id) ?? 0, need };
97	  }
98	
99	  /** GetSacrificeCount（cs:38-46） */
100	  getSacrificeCount(itemId: number): number {
101	    return this.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(itemId)?.have ?? 0;
102	  }
103	
104	  /** IsFullyResearched（cs:91-98） */
105	  isResearched(itemId: number): boolean {
106	    const n = this.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(itemId);
107	    return n !== null && n.have >= n.need;
108	  }
109	
110	  get lastEditId(): number { return this._lastEditId; }
111	
112	  // ---- 献祭 ----
113	  /**
114	   * RegisterItemSacrifice（cs:115-143）。
115	   * @returns 本次调用是否「刚研究完成」（跨过 need 线 = _newlyUnlocked 语义）；
116	   *          不可研究 / 已研究满 → false 且不产生任何状态变化（幂等）。
117	   */
118	  sacrifice(itemId: number, amount: number): boolean {
119	    if (!(amount > 0)) return false;
120	    const n = this.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(itemId);
121	    if (!n) return false;                       // 无 persistentId / 无 need 条目 → 早退
122	    const id = this.resolveId(itemId);
123	    if (n.have >= n.need) return false;         // cs:126-129 已满直接返回
124	    const value = Math.min(n.have + Math.floor(amount), n.need);
125	    this.counts.set(id, value);
126	    this.markContentsDirty();
127	    this.save();                                // 每次有效推进即落盘（原版由世界脏标记承担）
128	    return value >= n.need;
129	  }
130	
131	  /** SetSacrificeCountDirectly（cs:145-154）：0..9999 钳位，按解析后 id 记 */
132	  setSacrificeCountDirectly(itemId: number, sacrificeCount: number): void {
133	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(9999, Math.floor(sacrificeCount)));
134	    this.counts.set(this.resolveId(itemId), v);
135	    this.markContentsDirty();
136	    this.save();
137	  }
138	
139	  // ---- 统计（成就 45 门）----
140	  /** CountFullyResearchedItems（cs:59-74）：0..ItemID.Count-1 全域数 */
141	  countFullyResearchedItems(): SacrificeTotals {
142	    let fully = 0, all = 0;
143	    for (let i = 0; i < this.data.count; i++) {
144	      const n = this.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(i);
145	      if (!n) continue;
146	      all++;
147	      if (n.have >= n.need) fully++;
148	    }
149	    return { fullyResearchedItems: fully, allItems: all };
150	  }
151	
152	  /** 已研究完成数（allItems 的对偶，供 UI/成就门） */
153	  completedCount(): number { return this.countFullyResearchedItems().fullyResearchedItems; }
154	
155	  /** 可研究物品总数（≠ need 表长度：override 牺牲品可解析也计入——原版全域迭代语义） */
156	  totalItems(): number { return this.countFullyResearchedItems().allItems; }
157	
158	  /**
159	   * AchievementsHelper.CheckResearchAchievement（cs:387-398）1:1。
160	   * 版本门 + 整除半数门；过门 → 触发 onProgressionEvent(45)（成就 TRASH_COMPACTOR）。
161	   * @param forced 原 forced:true（Player.OnEnterWorld, Player.cs:318）
162	   * @returns 本次是否过半数门（true 即已 NotifyProgressionEvent(45)）
163	   */
164	  checkResearchAchievement(forced = false): boolean {
165	    if (!forced && this.achVersion === this._lastEditId) return false;
166	    this.achVersion = this._lastEditId;
167	    const { fullyResearchedItems, allItems } = this.countFullyResearchedItems();
168	    if (fullyResearchedItems >= Math.floor(allItems / 2)) {
169	      this.onProgressionEvent?.(45);
170	      return true;
171	    }
172	    return false;
173	  }
174	
175	  // ---- 持久化 ----
176	  private load(): void {
177	    const raw2 = this.store.load();
178	    if (!raw2) return;
179	    try {
180	      const data = JSON.parse(raw2) as { v?: number; c?: Record<string, number> };
181	      if (data.v !== 1 || !data.c) return;
182	      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(data.c)) {
183	        const id = Number(k);
184	        if (!Number.isInteger(id) || !(v >= 0)) continue;
185	        this.counts.set(id, Math.min(9999, Math.floor(v)));
186	      }
187	    } catch { /* 损坏静默丢弃 */ }
188	  }
189	
190	  private save(): void {
191	    const c: Record<string, number> = {};
192	    for (const [id, v] of this.counts) c[String(id)] = v;
193	    this.store.save(JSON.stringify({ v: 1, c }));
194	  }
195	
196	  /** Reset（cs:205-213）：清计数并置脏 */
197	  reset(): void {
198	    this.counts.clear();
199	    this.markContentsDirty();
200	    this.save();
201	  }
202	
203	  private markContentsDirty(): void { this._lastEditId++; }
204	}
205	

```

```
1	// Journey 研究（献祭）需求表提取（1.4.5.6 = Terarria1456）→ src/data/vanilla-research.json。
2	//
3	// 权威来源（反编译）：
4	//   1. Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent.Creative.Content.Sacrifices.tsv（6195 行）
5	//      —— CreativeItemSacrificesCatalog.Initialize（CreativeItemSacrificesCatalog.cs:14-69）
6	//      内嵌资源原文。逐行规则 1:1：
7	//        · 行首 "//" 跳过（:30-32）
8	//        · 按 '\t' 分列，列数 < 3 跳过（:36，array2.Length >= 3）
9	//        · 第 0 列 = ItemID 常量名，Search.TryGetId 解析失败跳过（:36）
10	//        · 第 1 列 ToLower 后查类别表（:40-58）：
11	//            ""/"a"=50  "b"=25  "c"=5  "d"=1  "e"=剔除(flag)  "f"=2  "g"=3
12	//            "h"=10  "i"=15  "j"=30  "k"=99  "l"=100  "m"=200  "n"=20  "o"=400
13	//            其余 → 原版直接 throw（:56）——提取器同样 fail fast。
14	//   2. Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ContentSamples.cs FillResearchItemOverrides（:884-892）
15	//      —— CreativeResearchItemPersistentIdOverride（ContentSamples.cs:823）。
16	//      AddItemResearchOverride(itemTypeToUnlock, params itemsThatWillResearchIt)
17	//      （:896-902）语义：献祭 params 里的物品 → 计入 unlock 物品的研究。
18	//      运行时三处（GetSacrificeCount :40 / TryGetSacrificeNumbers :78 / RegisterItemSacrifice
19	//      :117）都先走 override 再查 need 表，因此计数器按「解析后 id」共享。
20	//   3. Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ItemID.cs（:1443-13833，public const short NAME = id;）
21	//      —— 名称 → net id；Count = 6147（:13833）。
22	//        CountFullyResearchedItems（ItemsSacrificedUnlocksTracker.cs:59-74）按
23	//        0..ItemID.Count-1 逐 id 调 TryGetSacrificeNumbers 计 allItems（override
24	//        解析成功的牺牲品也计入）——Research.ts 同法，不能用 need 表长度近似。
25	//
26	// 产物结构：{ v, meta:{源行号锚点}, count, need:{id:数量}, override:{牺牲id:解锁id} }
27	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
28	
29	const ROOT = '~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld';
30	const read = (p) => readFileSync(`${ROOT}/${p}`, 'utf8');
31	
32	/** 类别字母 → 需求数量（CreativeItemSacrificesCatalog.cs:25-58 switch 1:1）。
33	 *  值为 null = 类别 e（剔除，不入 need 表）。 */
34	const CATEGORY = {
35	  '': 50, a: 50, b: 25, c: 5, d: 1, e: null, f: 2, g: 3,
36	  h: 10, i: 15, j: 30, k: 99, l: 100, m: 200, n: 20, o: 400,
37	};
38	
39	// ---- ItemID.cs：名称 → id（public const short NAME = id;）----
40	const itemidSrc = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ItemID.cs');
41	const idByName = new Map();
42	const idRe = /public const short ([A-Za-z0-9_]+) = (-?\d+);/g;
43	for (const m of itemidSrc.matchAll(idRe)) {
44	  if (idByName.has(m[1])) throw new Error(`ItemID.cs duplicate name: ${m[1]}`);
45	  idByName.set(m[1], parseInt(m[2], 10));
46	}
47	const countMatch = itemidSrc.match(/public static readonly short Count = (\d+);/);
48	if (!countMatch) throw new Error('ItemID.Count not found');
49	const ITEM_COUNT = parseInt(countMatch[1], 10);
50	
51	// ---- Sacrifices.tsv：need 表 ----
52	const tsv = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.GameContent.Creative.Content.Sacrifices.tsv');
53	const need = {};
54	const hist = {};
55	let dataRows = 0, commentRows = 0, shortRows = 0, unknownNames = 0, excluded = 0;
56	for (const rawLine of tsv.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/)) {
57	  if (rawLine.startsWith('//')) { commentRows++; continue; }          // :30-32
58	  const cols = rawLine.split('\t');
59	  if (cols.length < 3) { shortRows++; continue; }                     // :36
60	  const name = cols[0];
61	  if (!idByName.has(name)) { unknownNames++; continue; }              // Search.TryGetId 失败跳过
62	  const cat = cols[1].toLowerCase();                                  // :40
63	  if (!(cat in CATEGORY)) throw new Error(`no category for ${name}: ${cols[1]}`);
64	  const value = CATEGORY[cat];
65	  hist[cat] = (hist[cat] ?? 0) + 1;
66	  dataRows++;
67	  if (value === null) { excluded++; continue; }                       // 'e' = flag 剔除
68	  const id = idByName.get(name);
69	  if (id < 0) { excluded++; continue; }                               // 负 id 不在 0..Count-1 域内
70	  need[String(id)] = value;
71	}
72	
73	// ---- ContentSamples.cs：研究 id 覆盖表（FillResearchItemOverrides :884-892）----
74	const csSrc = read('Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/ContentSamples.cs');
75	const fnMatch = csSrc.match(
76	  /private static void FillResearchItemOverrides\(\)\s*\{([\s\S]*?)\n\t\}/,
77	);
78	if (!fnMatch) throw new Error('FillResearchItemOverrides not found');
79	const override = {};
80	let overrideLines = 0;
81	for (const m of fnMatch[1].matchAll(/AddItemResearchOverride\((\d+),\s*([\d,\s]+)\);/g)) {
82	  const unlock = parseInt(m[1], 10);
83	  for (const s of m[2].split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10))) {
84	    if (!Number.isFinite(s)) continue;
85	    override[String(s)] = unlock;                                     // AddItemResearchOverride_Inner :906
86	    overrideLines++;
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	const out = {
91	  v: 1,
92	  meta: {
93	    source: 'Terarria1456 1.4.5.6',
94	    tsv: 'Terraria.GameContent.Creative.Content.Sacrifices.tsv',
95	    catalog: 'CreativeItemSacrificesCatalog.cs:14-69',
96	    overrideSrc: 'ContentSamples.cs FillResearchItemOverrides:884-892',
97	    tracker: 'ItemsSacrificedUnlocksTracker.cs',
98	    rows: { data: dataRows, comment: commentRows, short: shortRows, unknownName: unknownNames, excluded },
99	  },
100	  count: ITEM_COUNT,
101	  need,
102	  override,
103	};
104	writeFileSync(
105	  `${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-research.json`,
106	  JSON.stringify(out),
107	);
108	console.log(`need=${Object.keys(need).length} override=${overrideLines} count=${ITEM_COUNT} ` +
109	  `rows(data/comment/short/unknown/excluded)=${dataRows}/${commentRows}/${shortRows}/${unknownNames}/${excluded}`);
110	console.log('category histogram:', JSON.stringify(hist));
111	

```

```
99	  it('整数除法门：all=3 时完成 1 即过门（1 >= 3/2=1）', () => {
100	    const fired: number[] = [];
101	    const r = new Research(memStore(), smallData({ '1': 1, '2': 1, '3': 1 }));
102	    r.onProgressionEvent = (id) => fired.push(id);
103	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement(true)).toBe(false);   // 空库 forced
104	    expect(r.sacrifice(1, 1)).toBe(true);
105	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement()).toBe(true);
106	    expect(fired).toEqual([45]);                            // TRASH_COMPACTOR
107	  });
108	  it('未过半数不触发', () => {
109	    const fired: number[] = [];
110	    const r = new Research(memStore(), smallData({ '1': 1, '2': 1, '3': 1, '4': 1 }));
111	    r.onProgressionEvent = (id) => fired.push(id);
112	    r.sacrifice(1, 1);
113	    expect(r.completedCount()).toBe(1);
114	    expect(r.totalItems()).toBe(4);
115	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement()).toBe(false);       // 1 < 4/2=2
116	    expect(fired).toEqual([]);
117	  });

120	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement(true)).toBe(false);
121	    r.setSacrificeCountDirectly(1, 1);
122	    r.setSacrificeCountDirectly(2, 1);                      // lastEditId 停在新值
123	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement()).toBe(true);        // 版本变了 → 重数 → 过门
124	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement()).toBe(false);       // 版本未变 → 早退
125	    expect(r.checkResearchAchievement(true)).toBe(true);    // forced 重数
126	  });
127	});

129	describe('持久化（sbw.research.v1）', () => {
130	  it('计数跨实例存活；损坏数据静默丢弃', () => {
131	    const store = memStore();

133	    a.sacrifice(1, 2);

135	    expect(b.getSacrificeCount(1)).toBe(2);
136	    expect(b.lastEditId).toBe(0);                           // lastEditId 不持久（原版同样仅内存）
137	    store.save('not json');
138	    const c = new Research(store, smallData({ '1': 3 }));
139	    expect(c.getSacrificeCount(1)).toBe(0);
140	  });

144	    r.sacrifice(1, 1);
145	    r.reset();

147	    expect(r.isResearched(1)).toBe(false);

149	  });
```

```
30	.sw-res-prog { font-size:12px; color:#9fb0dd; margin-bottom:6px; }
31	.sw-res-list { overflow-y:auto; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:3px; }
32	.sw-res-row { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; padding:4px 6px;
33	  background:rgba(20,26,52,0.55); border-radius:4px; }

37	.sw-res-row .bar { width:90px; height:8px; background:#1a1f3a; border-radius:4px;
38	  border:1px solid #444c7a; overflow:hidden; flex:none; }
39	.sw-res-row .bar .fill { height:100%; background:linear-gradient(90deg,#6a8ae0,#8ad0e0); }
40	.sw-res-row .num { font-size:11px; color:#9fb0dd; width:64px; text-align:right; flex:none; }
41	.sw-res-row button { flex:none; margin:0; padding:3px 10px; width:auto; font-size:12px;
42	  background:linear-gradient(#4a3a6a,#3a2a5a); color:#fff; border:1px solid #6a5a8a;
43	  border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer; }
44	`;
45	
46	let cssInjected = false;
47	function ensureCss(): void {
48	  if (cssInjected) return;
49	  cssInjected = true;
50	  const s = document.createElement('style');
51	  s.textContent = CSS;
52	  document.head.appendChild(s);
53	}

57	
58	function el<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(tag: K, cls?: string, text?: string): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
59	  const e = document.createElement(tag);
60	  if (cls) e.className = cls;
61	  if (text !== undefined) e.textContent = text;
62	  return e;
63	}
64	
65	export function openResearchPanel(game: Game): void {
66	  ensureCss();
67	  if (panel) { closeResearchPanel(); return; }
68	  const r = game.research;

70	
71	  panel = el('div', 'sw-res-panel');
72	  const head = el('div', 'sw-res-head');
73	  head.appendChild(el('div', 'sw-title', Lang.text('LegacyInterface.112') !== 'LegacyInterface.112'
74	    ? Lang.text('LegacyInterface.112') : '研究'));   // Journey UI 研究
75	  const closeBtn = el('button', 'sw-btn', '✕');
76	  closeBtn.style.cssText = 'margin:0; padding:4px 10px; width:auto; font-size:12px; flex:none;';
77	  closeBtn.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); closeResearchPanel(); });
78	  head.appendChild(closeBtn);
79	  panel.appendChild(head);
80	
81	  const prog = el('div', 'sw-res-prog');
82	  panel.appendChild(prog);
83	  const list = el('div', 'sw-res-list');
84	  panel.appendChild(list);
85	
86	  const render = () => {
87	    // 全局进度（CountFullyResearchedItems）
88	    const done = r.completedCount();
89	    const total = r.totalItems();
90	    prog.textContent = `${Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Research.Progress')} ${done} / ${total}`;
91	    list.textContent = '';
92	    // 背包内可研究物品（去重聚堆）
93	    const seen = new Map<number, number>();
94	    for (const s of inv.slots) {
95	      if (!s) continue;
96	      const vid = vidOf(s.id);
97	      if (vid <= 0 || !r.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(vid)) continue;
98	      seen.set(vid, (seen.get(vid) ?? 0) + s.stack);
99	    }
100	    if (!seen.size) {
101	      list.appendChild(el('div', '', Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Research.Empty')));
102	      return;
103	    }
104	    for (const [vid, stack] of seen) {
105	      const info = r.tryGetSacrificeNumbers(vid)!;
106	      const doneItem = info.have >= info.need;
107	      const row = el('div', 'sw-res-row' + (doneItem ? ' done' : ''));
108	      const nm = el('div', 'nm', Lang.itemName(vid) ?? `#${vid}`);
109	      row.appendChild(nm);
110	      const bar = el('div', 'bar');
111	      const fill = el('div', 'fill');
112	      fill.style.width = `${Math.min(100, Math.round((info.have / info.need) * 100))}%`;
113	      bar.appendChild(fill);
114	      row.appendChild(bar);
115	      row.appendChild(el('div', 'num', doneItem ? '✓' : `${Math.min(info.have, info.need)}/${info.need}`));
116	      if (!doneItem) {
117	        // 背包按原版 id 扣除（Player.ConsumeItem 顺序扫描语义的 UI 侧等价）：
118	        // 从 slots 找到首个匹配 vid 的堆扣 1，返回是否成功
119	        const consumeOne = (): boolean => {
120	          for (let i = 0; i < inv.slots.length; i++) {
121	            const s = inv.slots[i];
122	            if (s && vidOf(s.id) === vid) {
123	              s.stack--;
124	              if (s.stack <= 0) inv.slots[i] = null;
125	              return true;
126	            }
127	          }
128	          return false;
129	        };
130	        const b1 = el('button', '', Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Research.Sacrifice'));
131	        b1.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
132	          e.stopPropagation();
133	          // RegisterItemSacrifice：扣 1 + 计数（幂等早退在内核侧）
134	          if (consumeOne()) {
135	            r.sacrifice(vid, 1);
136	            game.cb.onInventoryChanged?.();
137	          }
138	          render();
139	        });
140	        const bAll = el('button', '', Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Research.SacrificeAll'));
141	        bAll.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
142	          e.stopPropagation();
143	          let n = 0;
144	          while (n < stack && consumeOne()) { r.sacrifice(vid, 1); n++; }
145	          if (n > 0) game.cb.onInventoryChanged?.();
146	          render();
147	        });
148	        row.appendChild(b1);
149	        row.appendChild(bAll);
150	      }
151	      list.appendChild(row);
152	    }
153	  };
154	  render();
155	
156	  panel.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => e.stopPropagation());
157	  (document.querySelector('.sw-root') ?? document.body).appendChild(panel);
158	  escHandler = (e) => { if (e.code === 'Escape') closeResearchPanel(); };
159	  window.addEventListener('keydown', escHandler);
160	  game.sfx.play('menuTick', 0.6);
161	}
162	
163	export function closeResearchPanel(): void {
164	  panel?.remove();
165	  panel = null;
166	  if (escHandler) { window.removeEventListener('keydown', escHandler); escHandler = null; }
```

```
Key vanilla sacrifice semantics (CreativeUI.SacrificeItem :288-328):
- num = clamp(need - have, 0, need); if 0 → CannotSacrifice
- num2 = min(num, item.stack) — sacrifices AT MOST the remaining needed amount, never more
- item.stack -= num2 (excess returned to inventory if spawnExcessItem)
```

```json
		{
```

```
			{
				inventory[i].stack--;
				if (inventory[i].stack <= 0)

					inventory[i].SetDefaults(0);
				}
```
